## Paired-world uncertainty and supplemental direct contrasts

Intervals resample whole paired worlds 10,000 times with fixed analysis seed 20261107. They describe the empirical enrolled-world sample (held n=6 per stratum; pilots smaller) and are unstable at this size; they are not qualification, model calibration, clinical evidence or a promotion threshold. No individual is treated as an independent replicate. All successful enrolled worlds remain included.

Regional food shortfall measures availability, not every household's access. Retail access subtotals and unknown coverage are separate; `no_relief` changes the title fraction from 0.9 to 1 and uses L2's existing free-pool allocation without the retail means test, a joint ownership/allocation regime. Existing commons and public releases can still supply free rations. It creates no new food, and is not a pure price control. PH cost_only retains wages/supplies/guards and the chosen crowding basis, but responsive realized spending need not be equal. Classified displacement is not inferred physical crowding. Incomplete arrival diagnostics remain unknown ratios.
